    3671           0 : void BKE_object_apply_parent_inverse(Object *ob)
    3672             : {
    3673             :   /*
    3674             :    * Use parent's world transform as the child's origin.
    3675             :    *
    3676             :    * Let:
    3677             :    *    `local = identity`
    3678             :    *    `world = orthonormalized(parent)`
    3679             :    *
    3680             :    * Then:
    3681             :    *    `world = parent @ parentinv @ local`
    3682             :    *    `inv(parent) @ world = parentinv`
    3683             :    *    `parentinv = inv(parent) @ world`
    3684             :    *
    3685             :    * NOTE: If `ob->object_to_world().ptr()` has shear, then this `parentinv` is insufficient
    3686             :    * because `parent @ parentinv => shearless result`
    3687             :    *
    3688             :    *    Thus, local will have shear which cannot be decomposed into TRS:
    3689             :    *    `local = inv(parent @ parentinv) @ world`
    3690             :    *
    3691             :    *    This is currently not supported for consistency in the handling of shear during the other
    3692             :    *    parenting ops: Parent (Keep Transform), Clear [Parent] and Keep Transform.
    3693             :    */
    3694             :   float par_locrot[4][4], par_imat[4][4];
    3695           0 :   BKE_object_get_parent_matrix(ob, ob->parent, par_locrot);
    3696           0 :   invert_m4_m4(par_imat, par_locrot);
    3697             : 
    3698           0 :   orthogonalize_m4_stable(par_locrot, 0, true);
    3699             : 
    3700           0 :   mul_m4_m4m4(ob->parentinv, par_imat, par_locrot);
    3701             : 
    3702             :   /* Now, preserve `world` given the new `parentinv`.
    3703             :    *
    3704             :    * `world = parent @ parentinv @ local`
    3705             :    * `inv(parent) @ world = parentinv @ local`
    3706             :    * `inv(parentinv) @ inv(parent) @ world = local`
    3707             :    *
    3708             :    * `local = inv(parentinv) @ inv(parent) @ world`
    3709             :    */

    3711           0 :   copy_m4_m4(ob_local, ob->parentinv);
    3712           0 :   invert_m4(ob_local);
    3713           0 :   mul_m4_m4_post(ob_local, par_imat);
    3714           0 :   mul_m4_m4_post(ob_local, ob->object_to_world().ptr());
